It is provided as a reference template because\n     5\tpartial edits to the existing DE loop often result in broken syntax.\n     6\t\n     7\tJADE: Adaptive Differential Evolution with Optional External Archive\n     8\tReference: Zhang & Sanderson (2009)\n     9\t\"\"\"\n    10\t\n    11\tfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\n    12\timport numpy as np\n    13\timport os\n    14\timport sys\n    15\t\n    16\t_config_seed = None\n    17\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    18\tfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n    19\t\n    20\t\n    21\tdef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    22\t    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    23\t    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n    24\t        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    25\t\n    26\t    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n    27\t        if x.ndim == 1:\n    28\t            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n    29\t        result = problem.func(x)\n    30\t        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    31\t    else:\n    32\t        return float(problem(x))\n    33\t\n    34\t\n    35\tclass DEOptimizer:\n    36\t    \"\"\"JADE optimizer with the same interface as the original DEOptimizer.\"\"\"\n    37\t\n    38\t    def __init__(self, dim, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals,\n    39\t                 params=None, controller=None, recorder=None, swarm_size=None):\n    40\t        self.dim = int(dim)\n    41\t        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n    42\t        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n    43\t        self.controller = controller\n    44\t        self.recorder = recorder\n    45\t        self.stats = {}\n    46\t        # Default pop size 40 to match evaluator, or use swarm_size\n    47\t        self.pop_size = int(swarm_size) if swarm_size is not None else 40\n    48\t        # F and CR defaults\n    49\t        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.7))\n    50\t        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n    51\t\n    52\t        # Bounds\n    53\t        lb = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n    54\t        ub = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n    55\t        if lb.ndim == 0:\n    56\t            lb = np.full(self.dim, float(lb))\n    57\t        if ub.ndim == 0:\n    58\t            ub = np.full(self.dim, float(ub))\n    59\t        self.lb = lb\n    60\t        self.ub = ub\n    61\t\n    62\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None):\n    63\t        \"\"\"Run JADE until max_evals is reached.\n    64\t\n    65\t        Returns:\n    66\t            (gbest_list, stats_dict)\n    67\t        \"\"\"\n    68\t        dim = self.dim\n    69\t        lb = self.lb\n    70\t        ub = self.ub\n    71\t        pop_size = self.pop_size\n    72\t\n    73\t        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n    74\t        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n    75\t\n    76\t        # ---- Initialization ----\n    77\t        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, dim))\n    78\t        if x0 is not None:\n    79\t            x0_arr = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).flatten()\n    80\t            if x0_arr.shape[0] == dim:\n    81\t                pop[0] = np.clip(x0_arr, lb, ub)\n    82\t\n    83\t        evals = 0\n    84\t        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n    85\t        for i in range(pop_size):\n    86\t            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n    87\t                break\n    88\t            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n    89\t            evals += 1\n    90\t\n    91\t        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n    92\t        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n    93\t        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n    94\t        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n    95\t        prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n    96\t\n    97\t        # ---- JADE memory and archive ----\n    98\t        archive = np.empty((0, dim), dtype=float)\n    99\t        archive_capacity = max(pop_size * 2, 20)\n   100\t        mu_F = float(self.F)\n   101\t        mu_CR = float(self.CR)\n   102\t        c = 0.1\n   103\t\n   104\t        iteration = 0\n   105\t        while evals < self.max_evals:\n   106\t            iteration += 1\n   107\t\n   108\t            # Controller-provided F/CR are blended into the adaptation memory\n   109\t            params = dict(self.params)\n   110\t            if self.controller is not None:\n   111\t                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n   112\t                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   113\t                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n   114\t                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n   115\t                    params.update(step_params)\n   116\t            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n   117\t            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n   118\t            base_F = min(1.0, max(0.0, 0.5 * base_F + 0.5 * mu_F))\n   119\t            base_CR = min(1.0, max(0.0, 0.5 * base_CR + 0.5 * mu_CR))\n   120\t\n   121\t            # p-best selection pool (shrinks as budget is consumed)\n   122\t            progress = evals / max(1, self.max_evals)\n   123\t            p = max(0.05, 0.2 * (1.0 - progress))\n   124\t            n_pbest = max(2, int(pop_size * p))\n   125\t            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n   126\t\n   127\t            # Archive + population pool for x_r2\n   128\t            archive_pop = np.vstack([pop, archive]) if len(archive) > 0 else pop\n   129\t            n_arch = len(archive_pop)\n   130\t\n   131\t            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n   132\t            S_F = []\n   133\t            S_CR = []\n   134\t\n   135\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   136\t                # F_i drawn from Cauchy around mu_F, truncated\n   137\t                F_i = base_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\n   138\t                F_i = float(np.clip(F_i, 0.1, 1.0))\n   139\t                # CR_i drawn from Normal around mu_CR, truncated\n   140\t                CR_i = base_CR + 0.1 * rng.randn()\n   141\t                CR_i = float(np.clip(CR_i, 0.0, 1.0))\n   142\t\n   143\t                # Current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n   144\t                pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, n_pbest)]\n   145\t                r1 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n   146\t                while r1 == i:\n   147\t                    r1 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n   148\t                if n_arch > 1:\n   149\t                    r2 = rng.randint(0, n_arch)\n   150\t                    x_r2 = archive_pop[r2]\n   151\t                else:\n   152\t                    r2 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n   153\t                    while r2 == i or r2 == r1:\n   154\t                        r2 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n   155\t                    x_r2 = pop[r2]\n   156\t\n   157\t                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (pop[pbest_idx] - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - x_r2)\n   158\t\n   159\t                # Boundary repair: reflect\n   160\t                for _ in range(5):\n   161\t                    below = mutant < lb\n   162\t                    above = mutant > ub\n   163\t                    if not np.any(below) and not np.any(above):\n   164\t                        break\n   165\t                    mutant = np.where(below, 2 * lb - mutant, mutant)\n   166\t                    mutant = np.where(above, 2 * ub - mutant, mutant)\n   167\t                mutant = np.clip(mutant, lb, ub)\n   168\t\n   169\t                # Binomial crossover\n   170\t                j_rand = rng.randint(0, dim)\n   171\t                mask = rng.random(dim) < CR_i\n   172\t                mask[j_rand] = True\n   173\t                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\n   174\t\n   175\t                # Evaluation and selection\n   176\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   177\t                    break\n   178\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trial)\n   179\t                evals += 1\n   180\t\n   181\t                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n   182\t                    # Save the replaced individual to the external archive\n   183\t                    if len(archive) < archive_capacity:\n   184\t                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].reshape(1, -1)])\n   185\t                    else:\n   186\t                        ridx = rng.randint(0, archive_capacity)\n   187\t                        archive[ridx] = pop[i]\n   188\t                    new_pop[i] = trial\n   189\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   190\t                    S_F.append(F_i)\n   191\t                    S_CR.append(CR_i)\n   192\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   193\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   194\t                        gbest = trial.copy()\n   195\t\n   196\t            pop = new_pop\n   197\t\n   198\t            # Update success-history memory (Lehmer mean for F, arithmetic for CR)\n   199\t            if len(S_F) > 0:\n   200\t                S_F_arr = np.array(S_F)\n   201\t                S_CR_arr = np.array(S_CR)\n   202\t                denom = np.sum(S_F_arr)\n   203\t                if denom > 0:\n   204\t                    mu_F = (1.0 - c) * mu_F + c * np.sum(S_F_arr ** 2) / denom\n   205\t                mu_CR = (1.0 - c) * mu_CR + c * np.mean(S_CR_arr)\n   206\t                mu_F = float(np.clip(mu_F, 0.1, 1.0))\n   207\t                mu_CR = float(np.clip(mu_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\n   208\t\n   209\t            # Controller reward / transition storage\n   210\t            if self.controller is not None and train:\n   211\t                improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n   212\t                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n   213\t                            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   214\t                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n   215\t                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, (evals >= self.max_evals))\n   216\t            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n   217\t\n   218\t        self.stats = {\n   219\t            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n   220\t            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n   221\t            'evals': int(evals),\n   222\t            'iterations': int(iteration),\n   223\t            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n   224\t        }\n   225\t        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\n   226\t\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. It is provided as a reference template because\n     5\tpartial edits to the existing DE loop often result in broken syntax.\n     6\t\n     7\tJADE: Adaptive Differential Evolution with Optional External Archive\n     8\tReference: Zhang & Sanderson (2009)\n     9\t\"\"\"\n    10\t\n    11\tfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\n    12\timport numpy as np\n    13\timport os\n    14\timport sys\n    15\t\n    16\t_config_seed = None\n    17\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    18\tfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n    19\t\n    20\t\n    21\tdef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    22\t    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    23\t    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n    24\t        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    25\t\n    26\t    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n    27\t        if x.ndim == 1:\n    28\t            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n    29\t        result = problem.func(x)\n    30\t        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    31\t    else:\n    32\t        return float(problem(x))\n    33\t\n    34\t\n    35\tclass DEOptimizer:\n    36\t    \"\"\"JADE optimizer with the same interface as the original DEOptimizer.\"\"\"\n    37\t\n    38\t    def __init__(self, dim, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals,\n    39\t                 params=None, controller=None, recorder=None, swarm_size=None):\n    40\t        self.dim = int(dim)\n    41\t        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n    42\t        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n    43\t        self.controller = controller\n    44\t        self.recorder = recorder\n    45\t        self.stats = {}\n    46\t        # Default pop size 40 to match evaluator, or use swarm_size\n    47\t        self.pop_size = int(swarm_size) if swarm_size is not None else 40\n    48\t        # F and CR defaults\n    49\t        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.7))\n    50\t        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n    51\t\n    52\t        # Bounds\n    53\t        lb = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n    54\t        ub = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n    55\t        if lb.ndim == 0:\n    56\t            lb = np.full(self.dim, float(lb))\n    57\t        if ub.ndim == 0:\n    58\t            ub = np.full(self.dim, float(ub))\n    59\t        self.lb = lb\n    60\t        self.ub = ub\n    61\t\n    62\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None):\n    63\t        \"\"\"Run JADE until max_evals is reached.\n    64\t\n    65\t        Returns:\n    66\t            (gbest_list, stats_dict)\n    67\t        \"\"\"\n    68\t        dim = self.dim\n    69\t        lb = self.lb\n    70\t        ub = self.ub\n    71\t        pop_size = self.pop_size\n    72\t\n    73\t        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n    74\t        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n    75\t\n    76\t        # ---- Initialization ----\n    77\t        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, dim))\n    78\t        if x0 is not None:\n    79\t            x0_arr = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).flatten()\n    80\t            if x0_arr.shape[0] == dim:\n    81\t                pop[0] = np.clip(x0_arr, lb, ub)\n    82\t\n    83\t        evals = 0\n    84\t        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n    85\t        for i in range(pop_size):\n    86\t            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n    87\t                break\n    88\t            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n    89\t            evals += 1\n    90\t\n    91\t        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n    92\t        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n    93\t        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n    94\t        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n    95\t        prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n    96\t\n    97\t        # ---- JADE memory and archive ----\n    98\t        archive = np.empty((0, dim), dtype=float)\n    99\t        archive_capacity = max(pop_size * 2, 20)\n   100\t        mu_F = float(self.F)\n   101\t        mu_CR = float(self.CR)\n   102\t        c = 0.1\n   103\t\n   104\t        iteration = 0\n   105\t        while evals < self.max_evals:\n   106\t            iteration += 1\n   107\t\n   108\t            # Controller-provided F/CR are blended into the adaptation memory\n   109\t            params = dict(self.params)\n   110\t            if self.controller is not None:\n   111\t                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n   112\t                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   113\t                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n   114\t                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n   115\t                    params.update(step_params)\n   116\t            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n   117\t            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n   118\t            base_F = min(1.0, max(0.0, 0.5 * base_F + 0.5 * mu_F))\n   119\t            base_CR = min(1.0, max(0.0, 0.5 * base_CR + 0.5 * mu_CR))\n   120\t\n   121\t            # p-best selection pool (shrinks as budget is consumed)\n   122\t            progress = evals / max(1, self.max_evals)\n   123\t            p = max(0.05, 0.2 * (1.0 - progress))\n   124\t            n_pbest = max(2, int(pop_size * p))\n   125\t            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n   126\t\n   127\t            # Archive + population pool for x_r2\n   128\t            archive_pop = np.vstack([pop, archive]) if len(archive) > 0 else pop\n   129\t            n_arch = len(archive_pop)\n   130\t\n   131\t            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n   132\t            S_F = []\n   133\t            S_CR = []\n   134\t\n   135\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   136\t                # F_i drawn from Cauchy around mu_F, truncated\n   137\t                F_i = base_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\n   138\t                F_i = float(np.clip(F_i, 0.1, 1.0))\n   139\t                # CR_i drawn from Normal around mu_CR, truncated\n   140\t                CR_i = base_CR + 0.1 * rng.randn()\n   141\t                CR_i = float(np.clip(CR_i, 0.0, 1.0))\n   142\t\n   143\t                # Current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n   144\t                pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, n_pbest)]\n   145\t                r1 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n   146\t                while r1 == i:\n   147\t                    r1 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n   148\t                if n_arch > 1:\n   149\t                    r2 = rng.randint(0, n_arch)\n   150\t                    x_r2 = archive_pop[r2]\n   151\t                else:\n   152\t                    r2 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n   153\t                    while r2 == i or r2 == r1:\n   154\t                        r2 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n   155\t                    x_r2 = pop[r2]\n   156\t\n   157\t                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (pop[pbest_idx] - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - x_r2)\n   158\t\n   159\t                # Boundary repair: reflect\n   160\t                for _ in range(5):\n   161\t                    below = mutant < lb\n   162\t                    above = mutant > ub\n   163\t                    if not np.any(below) and not np.any(above):\n   164\t                        break\n   165\t                    mutant = np.where(below, 2 * lb - mutant, mutant)\n   166\t                    mutant = np.where(above, 2 * ub - mutant, mutant)\n   167\t                mutant = np.clip(mutant, lb, ub)\n   168\t\n   169\t                # Binomial crossover\n   170\t                j_rand = rng.randint(0, dim)\n   171\t                mask = rng.random(dim) < CR_i\n   172\t                mask[j_rand] = True\n   173\t                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\n   174\t\n   175\t                # Evaluation and selection\n   176\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   177\t                    break\n   178\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trial)\n   179\t                evals += 1\n   180\t\n   181\t                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n   182\t                    # Save the replaced individual to the external archive\n   183\t                    if len(archive) < archive_capacity:\n   184\t                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].reshape(1, -1)])\n   185\t                    else:\n   186\t                        ridx = rng.randint(0, archive_capacity)\n   187\t                        archive[ridx] = pop[i]\n   188\t                    new_pop[i] = trial\n   189\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   190\t                    S_F.append(F_i)\n   191\t                    S_CR.append(CR_i)\n   192\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   193\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   194\t                        gbest = trial.copy()\n   195\t\n   196\t            pop = new_pop\n   197\t\n   198\t            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\trm]\n   136\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   137\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   138\t\n   139\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   140\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   141\t\n   142\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   143\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   144\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   145\t        else:\n   146\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   147\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   148\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   149\t\n   150\t        if self.training:\n   151\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   152\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   153\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   154\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   155\t\n   156\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   157\t\n   158\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   159\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   160\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>"

p-best selection pool (shrinks as budget is consumed)\n   122\t            progress = evals / max(1, self.max_evals)\n   123\t            p = max(0.05, 0.2 * (1.0 - progress))\n   124\t            n_pbest = max(2, int(pop_size * p))\n   125\t            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n   126\t\n   127\t            # Boundary repair: reflect\n   160\t                for _ in range(5):\n   161\t                    below = mutant < lb\n   162\t                    above = mutant > ub\n   163\t                    if not np.any(below) and not np.any(above):\n   164\t                        break\n   165\t                    mutant = np.where(below, 2 * lb - mutant, mutant)\n   166\t                    mutant = np.where(above, 2 * ub - mutant, mutant)\n   167\t                mutant = np.clip(mutant, lb, ub)\n   168\t\n   169\t                # Binomial crossover\n   170\t                j_rand = rng.randint(0, dim)\n   171\t                mask = rng.random(dim) < CR_i\n   172\t                mask[j_rand] = True\n   173\t                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\n   174\t\n   175\t                # Evaluation and selection\n   176\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   177\t                    break\n   178\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trial)\n   179\t                evals += 1\n   180\t\n   181\t                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n   182\t                    # Save the replaced individual to the external archive\n   183\t                    if len(archive) < archive_capacity:\n   184\t                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].reshape(1, -1)])\n   185\t                    else:\n   186\t                        ridx = rng.randint(0, archive_capacity)\n   187\t                        archive[ridx] = pop[i]\n   188\t                    new_pop[i] = trial\n   189\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   190\t                    S_F.append(F_i)\n   191\t                    S_CR.append(CR_i)\n   192\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   193\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   194\t                        gbest = trial.copy()\n   195\t\n   196\t            pop = new_pop\n   197\t\n   198\t            # values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outputs Q-values for each discrete action; `step`\n   134\t    returns the greedy action as It is provided as a reference template because\n     5\tpartial edits to the existing DE loop often result in broken syntax.\n     6\t\n     7\tJADE: Adaptive Differential Evolution with Optional External Archive\n     8\tReference: Zhang & Sanderson (2009)\n     9\t\"\"\"\n    10\t\n    11\tfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\n    12\timport numpy as np\n    13\timport os\n    14\timport sys\n    15\t\n    16\t_config_seed = None\n    17\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    18\tfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n    19\t\n    20\t\n    21\tdef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    22\t    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    23\t    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n    24\t        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    25\t\n    26\t    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n    27\t        if x.ndim == 1:\n    28\t            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n    29\t        result = problem.func(x)\n    30\t        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    31\t    else:\n    32\t        return float(problem(x))\n    33\t\n    34\t\n    35\tclass DEOptimizer:\n    36\t    \"\"\"JADE optimizer with the same interface as the original DEOptimizer.\"\"\"\n    37\t\n    38\t    def __init__(self, dim, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals,\n    39\t                 params=None, controller=None, recorder=None, swarm_size=None):\n    40\t        self.dim = int(dim)\n    41\t        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n    42\t        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n    43\t        self.controller = controller\n    44\t        self.recorder = recorder\n    45\t        self.stats = {}\n    46\t        # Default pop size 40 to match evaluator, or use swarm_size\n    47\t        self.pop_size = int(swarm_size) if swarm_size is not None else 40\n    48\t        # F and CR defaults\n    49\t        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.7))\n    50\t        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n    51\t\n    52\t        # Bounds\n    53\t        lb = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n    54\t        ub = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n    55\t        if lb.ndim == 0:\n    56\t            lb = np.full(self.dim, float(lb))\n    57\t        if ub.ndim == 0:\n    58\t            ub = np.full(self.dim, float(ub))\n    59\t        self.lb = lb\n    60\t        self.ub = ub\n    61\t\n    62\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None):\n    63\t        \"\"\"Run JADE until max_evals is reached.\n    64\t\n    65\t        Returns:\n    66\t            (gbest_list, stats_dict)\n    67\t        \"\"\"\n    68\t        dim = self.dim\n    69\t        lb = self.lb\n    70\t        ub = self.ub\n    71\t        pop_size = self.pop_size\n    72\t\n    73\t        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n    74\t        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n    75\t\n    76\t        # ---- Initialization ----\n    77\t        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, dim))\n    78\t        if x0 is not None:\n    79\t            x0_arr = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).flatten()\n    80\t            if x0_arr.shape[0] == dim:\n    81\t                pop[0] = np.clip(x0_arr, lb, ub)\n    82\t\n    83\t        evals = 0\n    84\t        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n    85\t        for i in range(pop_size):\n    86\t            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n    87\t                break\n    88\t            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n    89\t            evals += 1\n    90\t\n    91\t        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n    92\t        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n    93\t        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n    94\t        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n    95\t        prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n    96\t\n    97\t        # Record initial population for statistics/score computation\n    98\t        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n    99\t        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n   100\t\n   101\t        # ---- JADE memory and archive ----\n   102\t        archive = np.empty((0, dim), dtype=float)\n   103\t        archive_capacity = max(pop_size * 2, 20)\n   104\t        mu_F = float(self.F)\n   105\t        mu_CR = float(self.CR)\n   106\t        c = 0.1\n   107\t\n   108\t        iteration = 0\n   109\t\n   110\t        # Linear Population Size Reduction (LPSR) for stronger convergence\n   111\t        pop_size_start = self.pop_size\n   112\t        pop_size_min = max(5, int(0.25 * self.pop_size))\n   113\t\n   114\t        while evals < self.max_evals:\n   115\t            iteration += 1\n   116\t\n   117\t            # Shrink population size linearly as budget is consumed\n   118\t            progress = evals / max(1, self.max_evals)\n   119\t            target_pop = max(pop_size_min, int(pop_size_start * (1.0 - progress)))\n   120\t            if target_pop < pop_size:\n   121\t                sort_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[:target_pop]\n   122\t                pop = pop[sort_idx].copy()\n   123\t                fitness = fitness[sort_idx].copy()\n   124\t                pop_size = target_pop\n   125\t\n   126\t            # Controller-provided F/CR are blended into the adaptation memory\n   127\t            params = dict(self.params)\n   128\t            if self.controller is not None:\n   129\t                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n   130\t                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   131\t                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n   132\t                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n   133\t                    params.update(step_params)\n   134\t            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n   135\t            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n   136\t            base_F = min(1.0, max(0.0, 0.5 * base_F + 0.5 * mu_F))\n   137\t            base_CR = min(1.0,\n   138\t<response clipped>\n   139\tnp.vstack([pop, archive]) if len(archive) > 0 else pop\n   140\t            n_arch = len(archive_pop)\n   141\t\n   142\t            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n   143\t            S_F = []\n   144\t            S_CR = []\n   145\t\n   146\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   147\t                # Current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n   155\t                pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, n_pbest)]\n   156\t                r1 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n   157\t                while r1 == i:\n   158\t                    r1 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n   159\t                if n_arch > 1:\n   160\t                    r2 = rng.randint(0, n_arch)\n   161\t                    x_r2 = archive_pop[r2]\n   162\t                else:\n   163\t                    r2 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n   164\t                    while r2 == i or r2 == r1:\n   165\t                        r2 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n   166\t                    x_r2 = pop[r2]\n   167\t\n   168\t                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (pop[pbest_idx] - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - x_r2)\n   169\t\n   170\t                # Boundary repair: reflect\n   171\t                for _ in range(5):\n   172\t                    below = mutant < lb\n   173\t                    above = mutant > ub\n   174\t                    if not np.any(below) and not np.any(above):\n   175\t                        break\n   176\t                    mutant = np.where(below, 2 * lb - mutant, mutant)\n   177\t                    mutant = np.where(above, 2 * ub - mutant, mutant)\n   178\t                mutant = np.clip(mutant, lb, ub)\n   179\t\n   180\t                # Binomial crossover\n   181\t                j_rand = rng.randint(0, dim)\n   182\t                mask = rng.random(dim) < CR_i\n   183\t                mask[j_rand] = True\n   184\t                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\n   185\t\n   186\t                # Evaluation and selection\n   187\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   188\t                    break\n   189\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trial)\n   190\t                evals += 1\n   191\t\n   192\t                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n   193\t                    # Save the replaced individual to the external archive\n   194\t                    if len(archive) < archive_capacity:\n   195\t                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].reshape(1, -1)])\n   196\t                    else:\n   197\t                        ridx = rng.randint(0, archive_capacity)\n   198\t                        archive[ridx] = pop[i]\n   199\t                    new_pop[i] = trial\n   200\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   201\t                    S_F.append(F_i)\n   202\t                    S_CR.append(CR_i)\n   203\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   204\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   205\t                        gbest = trial.copy()\n   206\t\n   207\t            pop = new_pop\n   208\t\n   209\t            # Update success-history memory (Lehmer mean for F, arithmetic for CR)\n   210\t            if len(S_F) > 0:\n   211\t                S_F_arr = np.array(S_F)\n   212\t                S_CR_arr = np.array(S_CR)\n   213\t                denom = np.sum(S_F_arr)\n   214\t                if denom > 0:\n   215\t                    mu_F = (1.0 - c) * mu_F + c * np.sum(S_F_arr ** 2) / denom\n   216\t                mu_CR = (1.0 - c) * mu_CR + c * np.mean(S_CR_arr)\n   217\t                mu_F = float(np.clip(mu_F, 0.1, 1.0))\n   218\t                mu_CR = float(np.clip(mu_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\n   219\t\n   220\t            # Population restart/refill for additional diversity\n   221\t            if iteration % 20 == 0 and evals < self.max_evals:\n   222\t                n_refill = max(1, int(pop_size * 0.3))\n   223\t                refill_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[-n_refill:]\n   224\t                fresh = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (n_refill, dim))\n   225\t                for k, idx in enumerate(refill_idx):\n   226\t                    if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   227\t                        break\n   228\t                    pop[idx] = fresh[k]\n   229\t                    fitness[idx] = evaluate_problem(problem, fresh[k])\n   230\t                    evals += 1\n   231\t                    if fitness[idx] < gbest_f:\n   232\t                        gbest_f = fitness[idx]\n   233\t                        gbest = fresh[k].copy()\n   234\t\n   235\t            # Controller reward / transition storage\n   236\t            if self.controller is not None and train:\n   237\t                improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n   238\t                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n   239\t                            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   240\t                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n   241\t                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, (evals >= self.max_evals))\n   242\t            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n   243\t\n   244\t        self.stats = {\n   245\t            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n   246\t            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n   247\t            'evals': int(evals),\n   248\t            'iterations': int(iteration),\n   249\t            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n   250\t            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n   251\t            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n   252\t        }\n   253\t        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\n   254\t\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

It is provided as a reference template because\n     5\tpartial edits to the existing DE loop often result in broken syntax.\n     6\t\n     7\tJADE: Adaptive Differential Evolution with Optional External Archive\n     8\tReference: Zhang & Sanderson (2009)\n     9\t\"\"\"\n    10\t\n    11\tfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\n    12\timport numpy as np\n    13\timport os\n    14\timport sys\n    15\t\n    16\t_config_seed = None\n    17\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    18\tfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n    19\t\n    20\t\n    21\tdef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    22\t    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    23\t    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n    24\t        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    25\t\n    26\t    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n    27\t        if x.ndim == 1:\n    28\t            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n    29\t        result = problem.func(x)\n    30\t        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    31\t    else:\n    32\t        return float(problem(x))\n    33\t\n    34\t\n    35\tclass DEOptimizer:\n    36\t    \"\"\"JADE optimizer with the same interface as the original DEOptimizer.\"\"\"\n    37\t\n    38\t    def __init__(self, dim, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals,\n    39\t                 params=None, controller=None, recorder=None, swarm_size=None):\n    40\t        self.dim = int(dim)\n    41\t        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n    42\t        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n    43\t        self.controller = controller\n    44\t        self.recorder = recorder\n    45\t        self.stats = {}\n    46\t        # Default pop size 40 to match evaluator, or use swarm_size\n    47\t        self.pop_size = int(swarm_size) if swarm_size is not None else 40\n    48\t        # F and CR defaults\n    49\t        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.7))\n    50\t        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n    51\t\n    52\t        # Bounds\n    53\t        lb = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n    54\t        ub = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n    55\t        if lb.ndim == 0:\n    56\t            lb = np.full(self.dim, float(lb))\n    57\t        if ub.ndim == 0:\n    58\t            ub = np.full(self.dim, float(ub))\n    59\t        self.lb = lb\n    60\t        self.ub = ub\n    61\t\n    62\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None):\n    63\t        \"\"\"Run JADE until max_evals is reached.\n    64\t\n    65\t        Returns:\n    66\t            (gbest_list, stats_dict)\n    67\t        \"\"\"\n    68\t        dim = self.dim\n    69\t        lb = self.lb\n    70\t        ub = self.ub\n    71\t        pop_size = self.pop_size\n    72\t\n    73\t        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n    74\t        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n    75\t\n    76\t        # ---- Initialization ----\n    77\t        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, dim))\n    78\t        if x0 is not None:\n    79\t            x0_arr = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).flatten()\n    80\t            if x0_arr.shape[0] == dim:\n    81\t                pop[0] = np.clip(x0_arr, lb, ub)\n    82\t\n    83\t        evals = 0\n    84\t        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n    85\t        for i in range(pop_size):\n    86\t            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n    87\t                break\n    88\t            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n    89\t            evals += 1\n    90\t\n    91\t        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n    92\t        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n    93\t        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n    94\t        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n    95\t        prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n    96\t\n    97\t        # Record initial population for statistics/score computation\n    98\t        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n    99\t        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n   100\t\n   101\t        #
